<td valign="top"><strong>Question:</strong> Which metal ion is the cofactor associated with this protein’s phosphatidylglycerophosphate synthase activity?<br><br><strong>Choices:</strong><br><br><strong>A.</strong> Mg²⁺<br><br><strong>B.</strong> Mn²⁺<br><br><strong>C.</strong> Zn²⁺<br><br><strong>D.</strong> Ca²⁺<br><br><strong>Flash-Next response:</strong><br><br><br><br>## Reasoning:<br><br>**Step 1: Identify the protein**<br>The sequence shows characteristics of a mitochondrial inner membrane protein with an N-terminal mitochondrial targeting sequence (enriched in serines, arginines, and hydroxylated residues), followed by multiple transmembrane domains. The question states it has phosphatidylglycerophosphate synthase activity.<br><br>**Step 2: Identify the enzymatic activity**<br>Phosphatidylglycerophosphate synthase is CDP-diacylglycerol:sn-glycerol-3-phosphate 3-phosphatidyltransferase (EC 2.7.8.5). This enzyme catalyzes the formation of phosphatidylglycerophosphate (PGP) from CDP-diacylglycerol and glycerol-3-phosphate.
